                        Viṣṇusmṛti (Viṣ)

Edited by V- Krishnamacharya-  The Adyar Library Series, vol- 93 (in 2 parts)-
Madras: The Adyar Library and Research Center, 1964-

	Computeri;sed by I- Shima, and collated by T- Hayashi for Ikari Project-
	Kyoto: Jinbunken (Kyoto University), august 1991-

N-B-
The Adyar Library edition is denoted by A- in square brackets for apparatus-
The sign "\" indicates a verb-
The negative prefix (a-/an-) is, as a rule, separated from the main part by a hyphen (e-g- a-mṛta)-

  VisnuSm_1
VisnuSm 1-01a/ brahma-rātryāṃ vyatītāyāṃ prabuddhe padma-saṃbhave /
VisnuSm 1-01c/ viṣṇuḥ sisṛkṣur\ bhūtāni j;nātvā\ bhūmiṃ jala-anugām //
VisnuSm 1-02a/ jala-krīḍā-ruci ;subhaṃ kalpa-ādhiṣu yathā purā / 
VisnuSm 1-02c/ vārāham āsthito rūpam ujjahāra\ vasuṃdharām //
VisnuSm 1-03a/ veda-pādo yūpa-daṃṣṭraḥ kratu-danta;s citī-mukhaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-03c/ agni-jihvo darbha-romā brahma-;sīrṣo mahā-tapāḥ //
VisnuSm 1-04a/ aho-rātra-īkṣaṇo divyo veda-a:nga-;sruti-bhūṣaṇaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-04c/ ājya-nāsaḥ sruva-tuṇḍaḥ sāma-ghoṣa-svano mahān //
VisnuSm 1-05a/ dharma-satya-mayaḥ ;srīmān krama-vikrama-satkṛtaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-05c/ prāya;scitta-mahā-ghoṇaḥ pa;su-jānur mahā-kṛtiḥ //
VisnuSm 1-06a/ udgātra-antro homa-li:ngo bīja-oṣadhi-mahā-phalaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-06c/ vedy-antara-ātmā mantra-sphig-vikṛtaḥ soma-;soṇitaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-07a/ vedi-skandho havir gandho havya-kavya-ādi-vega-vān /
VisnuSm 1-07c/ prāgvaṃṣa-kāyo dyuti-mān nānā-dīkṣābhir anvitaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-08a/ dakṣiṇā-hṛdayo yoga-mahā-mantra-mayo mahān /
VisnuSm 1-08c/ upākarma-uṣṭha-ruciraḥ pravargya-āvarta-bhūṣaṇaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-09a/ nānā-cchando-gati-patho guhya-upaniṣad-āsanaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-09c/ chāyā-patnī-sahāyo vai maṇi-;sṛ:nga iva^uditaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-10a/ mahīṃ sāgara-paryantāṃ sa-;saila-vana-kānanāṃ /
VisnuSm 1-10c/ eka-arṇava-jala-bhraṣṭām eka-arṇava-gataḥ prabhuḥ //
VisnuSm 1-11a/ daṃṣṭra-agreṇa samuddhṛtya lokānāṃ hita-kāmyayā /
VisnuSm 1-11c/ ādi-devo mahā-yogī cakāra\ jagatīṃ punaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-12a/ evaṃ yaj;na-varāheṇa bhūtvā bhūta-hita-arthinā /
VisnuSm 1-12c/ uddhṛtā pṛthivī devī rasātala-gatā purā //
VisnuSm 1-13a/ uddhṛtya\ ni;scale sthāne sthāpayitvā\ tathā svake /
VisnuSm 1-13c/ yathā-sthānaṃ vibhajya\^āpas tad-gatā madhusūdanaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-14a/ sāmudrya;s ca samudreṣu nādeyī;s ca nadīṣu ca /
VisnuSm 1-14c/ palvaleṣu ca pālvalyaḥ saraḥsu ca saro-bhavāḥ //
VisnuSm 1-15a/ pātāla-saptakaṃ cakre\ lokānāṃ saptakaṃ tathā /
VisnuSm 1-15c/ dvīpānām udadhīnāṃ ca sthānāni vividhāni ca //
VisnuSm 1-16a/ sthāna-pālān loka-pālān nadīḥ ;saila-vanaspatīn /
VisnuSm 1-16c/ ṛṣīṃ;s ca sapta dharma-j;nān vedān sa-an:nān sura-asurān //
VisnuSm 1-17a/ pi;sāca-uraga-gandharva-yakṣa-rākṣasa-mānuṣān /
VisnuSm 1-17c/ pa;su-pakṣi-mṛga-ādyāṃ;s ca bhūta-grāmaṃ catur-vidham /
VisnuSm 1-17e/ megha-indra-cāpa-;sampā-ādyān yaj;nāṃ;s ca vividhāṃs tathā //
VisnuSm 1-18a/ evaṃ varāho bhagavān kṛtvā\^idaṃ sa-cara-acaram /
VisnuSm 1-18c/ jagaj jagāma\ lokānām avij;nātāṃ tadā gatim //
VisnuSm 1-19a/ avij;nātāṃ gatiṃ yāte\ deva-deve janārdane /
VisnuSm 1-19c/ vasudhā cintayām āsa\ kā dhṛtir me bhaviṣyati\ //
VisnuSm 1-20a/ pṛcchāmi\ ka;syapaṃ gatvā sa me (vakṣyaty a-saṃ;sayam /
VisnuSm 1-20c/ madīyāṃ (vahate cintāṃ nityam eva mahā-muniḥ //
VisnuSm 1-21a/ evaṃ sā ni;scayaṃ kṛtvā devī strī-rūpa-dhāriṇī /
VisnuSm 1-21c/ jagāma\ ka;syapaṃ draṣṭuṃ dṛṣṭavāṃs tāṃ ca ka;syapaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-22a/ nīla-pa:nka-ja-patra-akṣīṃ ;sārada-indu-nibha-ānanām /
VisnuSm 1-22c/ ali-saṃghāla-kāṃ ;subhrāṃ bandhu-jīvā-dharāṃ ;subhām //
VisnuSm 1-23a/ su-bhrūṃ su-sūkṣma-da;sanāṃ cāru-nāsāṃ nata-bhruvam /
VisnuSm 1-23c/ kambu-kaṇṭhīṃ saṃhata-ūrūṃ pīna-ūrujaghana-sthalām /
VisnuSm 1-24a/ virejatuḥ\ stanau yasyāḥ samau pīnau nirantarau /
VisnuSm 1-24c/ ;sakra-ibha-kumbha-saṃkā;sau ;sāta-kumbha-sama-dyutī //
VisnuSm 1-25a/ mṛṇāla-komalau bāhū karau kisalaya-upamau /
VisnuSm 1-25c/ rukma-stambha-nibhāv ūrū gūḍhe ;sliṣṭe ca jānunī //
VisnuSm 1-26a/ ja:nghe virome su-same pādāv ati-mano-ramau /
VisnuSm 1-26c/ jaghanaṃ ca ghanaṃ madhyaṃ yathā kesariṇaḥ ;si;soḥ //
VisnuSm 1-27a/ prabhā-yutā nakhās tāmrā rūpaṃ sarva-mano-haram /
VisnuSm 1-27c/ kurvāṇāṃ vīkṣitair nityaṃ nīla-utpala-yutā di;saḥ //
VisnuSm 1-28a/ kurvāṇāṃ prabhayā devīṃ tathā vitimirā di;saḥ /  
VisnuSm 1-28c/ su-sūkṣma-;sukla-vasanāṃ ratna-uttama-vibhūṣitāṃ //
VisnuSm 1-29a/ pada-nyāsair vasu-matīṃ sa-padmām iva kurvatīṃ /
VisnuSm 1-29c/ rūpa-yauvana-saṃpannāṃ vinīta-vad upasthitām //
VisnuSm 1-30a/ samīpam āgatāṃ dṛṣṭvā\ pūjayitvā\^atha ka;syapaḥ /
VisnuSm 1-30c/ uvāca\ tāṃ vara-ārohe vij;nātaṃ hṛd-gataṃ mayā //
VisnuSm 1-31a/ dhare tava vi;sāla-akṣi gaccha\ devi janārdanam /
VisnuSm 1-31c/ sa te vakṣyaty\ a;seṣeṇa bhāvinī te yathā dhṛtiḥ //
VisnuSm 1-32a/ kṣīra-ude vasatis tasya mayā j;nātā ;subha-ānane /
VisnuSm 1-32c/ dhyāna-yogena cārv-a:ngi tvad-arthaṃ tat-prasādataḥ //
VisnuSm 1-33a/ ity evam uktā saṃpūjya\ ka;syapaṃ vasudhā tataḥ /
VisnuSm 1-33c/ prayayau\ ke;savaṃ draṣṭuṃ\ kṣīra-udam atha sāgaram //
VisnuSm 1-34a/ sā dadar;sa\^amṛta-nidhiṃ candra-ra;smi-mano-haram /
VisnuSm 1-34c/ pavana-kṣobha-saṃjāta-vīcī-;sata-samākulam //
VisnuSm 1-35a/ himavac-chata-saṃkā;saṃ bhū-maṇḍalam iva^aparam //
VisnuSm 1-35c/ vīcī-hastaiḥ pracalitair āhvayānam iva kṣitim //
VisnuSm 1-36a/ tair eva ;suklatāṃ candre vidadhānam iva^ani;sam /
VisnuSm 1-36c/ antara-sthena hariṇā vigata-a;seṣa-kalmaṣam //
VisnuSm 1-37a/ yasmāt tasmād dhārayantaṃ su-;suklāṃ tanum ūrjitām /
VisnuSm 1-37c/ pāṇḍuraṃ kha-gama-agamyam adho-bhuvana-vartinam //
VisnuSm 1-38a/ indra-nīla-kaḍāra-āḍhyaṃ viparītam iva^ambaram /
VisnuSm 1-38c/ phala-āvalī-samudbhūta-vana-saṃgham iva^ācitam //
VisnuSm 1-39a/ nirmokam iva ;seṣa-aher vistīrṇa-antam atīva hi /
VisnuSm 1-39c/ taṃ dṛṣṭvā\ tatra madhya-sthaṃ daḍṛ;se\ ke;sava-ālayam //
VisnuSm 1-40a/ anirde;sya-parīmāṇam anirde;sya-ṛddhi-saṃyutam /
VisnuSm 1-40c/ ;seṣa-parya:nka-gaṃ tasmin dadar;sa\ madhu-sūdanam //
VisnuSm 1-41a/ ;seṣa-ahi-phaṇa-ratna-aṃ;su-dur-vibhāvya-mukha-ambujam /
VisnuSm 1-41c/ ;sa;sa-a:nka-;sata-saṃkā;saṃ sūrya-ayuta-sama-prabham //
VisnuSm 1-42a/ pīta-vāsa-sama-kṣobhyaṃ sarva-ratna-vibhūṣitam / [savaratna- in ā]
VisnuSm 1-42c/ mukuṭena^arka-varṇena kuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam //
VisnuSm 1-43a/ saṃvāhyamāna-a:nghri-yugaṃ lakṣmyā kara-talaiḥ ;subhaiḥ /
VisnuSm 1-43c/ ;sarīra-dhāribhiḥ ;sastraiḥ sevyamānaṃ samantataḥ //
VisnuSm 1-44a/ taṃ dṛṣṭvā puṇḍarīka-akṣaṃ vavande\ madhu-sūdanam /
VisnuSm 1-44c/ jānubhyām avaniṃ gatvā vij;nāpayati\ ca^apy atha //
VisnuSm 1-45a/ uddhṛtā^ahaṃ tvayā deva rasātala-talaṃ gatā /
VisnuSm 1-45c/ sva-sthāne sthāpitā viṣṇo lokānāṃ hita-kāmyayā //
VisnuSm 1-46a/ tatra^adhunā hi deva-ī;sa kā dhṛtir me bhaviṣyati\ /
VisnuSm 1-46c/ evam uktas tayā devyā devo vacanam abravīt //
VisnuSm 1-47a/ varṇa-ā;srama-ācāra-ratāḥ santaḥ ;sāstra-eka-tat-parāḥ /
VisnuSm 1-47c/ tvāṃ dhare dhārayiṣyanti\ teṣāṃ tvad-bhāra āhitaḥ //
VisnuSm 1-48a/ evam uktā vasu-matī deva-devam abhāṣata /
VisnuSm 1-48c/ varṇānām ā;sramāṇāṃ ca dharmān vada sanātana //
VisnuSm 1-49a/ tvatto^aham ;srotum\ icchāmi\ tvaṃ hi me paramā gatiḥ //
VisnuSm 1-49c/ namas te deva-deva-ī;sa deva-ari-bala-sūdana //
VisnuSm 1-50a/ nārāyaṇa jagan-nātha ;sa:nkha-cakra-gadā-dhara /
VisnuSm 1-50c/ padma-nābha hṛṣīke;sa mahā-bala-parākrama //
VisnuSm 1-51a/ ati-indriya su-duṣ-pāra deva ;sār:nga-dhanur-dhara /
VisnuSm 1-51c/ varāha bhīma govinda purāṇa puruṣa-uttama //
VisnuSm 1-52a/ hiraṇya-ke;sa vi;sva-akṣa yaj;na-mūrte nir-a;njana /
VisnuSm 1-52c/ kṣetra-kṣetra-j;na-deva-ī;sa salila-arṇava-;sāyaka //
VisnuSm 1-53a/ mantra mantra-vaha^acintya veda-veda-a:nga-vigraha /
VisnuSm 1-53c/ jagato^asya samagrasya sṛṣṭi-saṃhāra-kāraka //
VisnuSm 1-54a/ dharma-adharma-j;na dharma-a:nga dharma-yone vara-prada /
VisnuSm 1-54c/ viṣvak-sena^amṛta vyoma madhu-kaiṭabha-sūdana //
VisnuSm 1-55a/ bṛhatāṃ bṛṃhaṇa^aj;neya sarva sarva-abhaya-prada /
VisnuSm 1-55c/ vareṇya^anagha jīmūta jagan-nirmāṇa-kāraka //
VisnuSm 1-56a/ āpyāyana apāṃ sthāna caitanya-ādhāra niṣkriya /
VisnuSm 1-56c/ sapta-;sīrṣa-adhvara-guro purāṇa-puruṣa-uttama //
VisnuSm 1-57a/ dhruva^akṣara su-sūkṣma-ī;sa bhakta-vatsala pāvana /
VisnuSm 1-57c/ tvaṃ gatiḥ sarva-devānāṃ tvaṃ gatir brahma-vādinām //
VisnuSm 1-58a/ tathā vidita-vedyānāṃ gatis tvaṃ puruṣa-uttama /
VisnuSm 1-58c/ prapannā^asmi\ jagan-nātha dhruvaṃ vācaspatiṃ prabhum //
VisnuSm 1-59a/ su-brahmaṇyam an-ādhṛṣyaṃ vasu-ṣeṇaṃ vasu-pradam /
VisnuSm 1-59c/ mahā-yoga-bala-upetaṃ pṛ;sni-garbhaṃ dhṛta-arciṣam //
VisnuSm 1-60a/ vāsudevaṃ mahā-ātmānaṃ puṇḍarīka-akṣaṃ acyutam /
VisnuSm 1-60c/ sura-asura-guruṃ devaṃ vibhuṃ bhūta-mahā-ī;svaram //
VisnuSm 1-61a/ eka-vyūhaṃ catur-bāhuṃ jagat-kāraṇa-kāraṇam /
VisnuSm 1-61c/ brūhi\ me bhagavan dharmāṃ;s cāturvarṇyasya ;sā;svatān //
VisnuSm 1-62a/ ā;srama-ācāra-saṃyuktān sa-rahasyān sa-saṃgrahān /
VisnuSm 1-62c/ evam uktas tu deva-ī;saḥ kṣoṇyā kṣoṇīm abhāṣata //
VisnuSm 1-63a/ ;sṛṇu devi dhare dharmāṃ;s cāturvarṇyasya ;sā;svatān /
VisnuSm 1-63c/ ā;srama-ācāra-saṃyuktān sa-rahasyān sa-saṃgrahān //
VisnuSm 1-64a/ ye tu tvāṃ dhārayiṣyanti\ santas teṣāṃ parāyaṇān /
VisnuSm 1-64c/ niṣaṇṇā bhava vāma-ūru kā;ncane^asmin vara-āsane //
VisnuSm 1-65a/ sukha-āsīnā nibodha tvaṃ dharmān nigadato mama /
VisnuSm 1-65c/ ;su;sruve\ vaiṣṇavān dharmān sukha-āsīnā dharā tadā //

VisnuSm 2-01 om / brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vai;syaḥ ;sūdra;s ca^iti varṇā;s catvāraḥ //
VisnuSm 2-02 teṣām ādyā dvi-jātayas trayaḥ //
VisnuSm 2-03 teṣāṃ niṣeka-ādyaḥ ;sma;sāna-anto mantra-vat kriyā-samūhaḥ //
VisnuSm 2-04 teṣāṃ ca dharmāḥ //
VisnuSm 2-05 brāhmaṇasya^adhyāpanam //
VisnuSm 2-06 kṣatriyasya ;sastra-nityatā //
VisnuSm 2-07 vai;syasya pa;su-pālanam //
VisnuSm 2-08 ;sūdrasya dvi-jāti-;su;srūṣā //
VisnuSm 2-09 dvijānāṃ yajana-adhyayane //
VisnuSm 2-10 atha^eteṣāṃ vṛttayaḥ //
VisnuSm 2-11 brāhmaṇasya yājana-pratigrahau //
VisnuSm 2-12 kṣatriyasya kṣiti-trāṇam //
VisnuSm 2-13 kṛṣi-go-rakṣa-vāṇijya-kusīda-yoni-poṣaṇāni vai;syasya //
VisnuSm 2-14 ;sūdrasya sarva-;silpāni //
VisnuSm 2-15 āpady anantarā vṛttiḥ //
VisnuSm 2-16a/ kṣamā satyam damaḥ ;saucaṃ dānam indriya-saṃyamaḥ /
VisnuSm 2-16c/ ahiṃsā guru-;su;srūṣā tīrtha-anusaraṇaṃ dayā //
VisnuSm 2-17a/ ārjavaṃ lobha-;sūnyatvaṃ deva-brāhmaṇa-pūjanam /
VisnuSm 2-17c/ an-abhyasūyā ca tathā dharmaḥ sāmānya ucyate\ //
  VisnuSm_3
VisnuSm 3-01 atha rāja-dharmāḥ //
VisnuSm 3-02 prajā-paripālanam //
VisnuSm 3-03 varṇa-ā;sramāṇāṃ sve sve dharme vyavasthāpanam //
VisnuSm 3-04 rājā ca jā:ngalaṃ pa;savyaṃ sasya-upetaṃ de;sam ā;srayet\//
VisnuSm 3-05 vai;sya-;sūdra-prāyaṃ ca //
VisnuSm 3-06 tatra dhanva-nṛ-mahī-vāri-vṛkṣa-giri-durgāṇām anyatamaṃ durgam ā;srayet\ //
VisnuSm 3-07 tatra-stha;s ca sva-sva-grāma-adhipān kuryāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-08 da;sa-adhyakṣān //
VisnuSm 3-09 ;sata-adhyakṣān //
VisnuSm 3-10 de;sa-adhyakṣāṃ;s ca //
VisnuSm 3-11 grāma-doṣāṇāṃ grāma-adhyakṣaḥ parihāraṃ kuryāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-12 a-;sakto da;sa-grāma-adhyakṣāya nivedayet\ //
VisnuSm 3-13 so^apy a-;saktaḥ ;sata-adhyakṣāya //
VisnuSm 3-14 so^apy a-;sakto de;sa-adhyakṣāya //
VisnuSm 3-15 de;sa-adhyakṣo^api sarva-ātmanā doṣam ucchindyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-16 ākara-;sulka-tara-nāga-vane;sv āptān niyu;njīta\ //
VisnuSm 3-17 dharmiṣṭhān dharma-kāryeṣu //
VisnuSm 3-18 nipuṇān artha-kāryeṣu //
VisnuSm 3-19 ;sūrān saṃgrāma-karmasu //
VisnuSm 3-20 ugrān ugreṣu //
VisnuSm 3-21 ṣaṇḍhān strīṣu //
VisnuSm 3-22 prajābhyo baly-arthaṃ saṃvatsareṇa dhānyataḥ ṣaṣṭham aṃ;sam ādadyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-23 sarva-sasyebhya;s ca //
VisnuSm 3-24 dvikaṃ ;sataṃ pa;su-hiraṇyebhyo vastrebhya;s ca //
VisnuSm 3-25 māṃsa-madhu-ghṛta-oṣadhi-gandha-puṣpa-mūla-phala-rasa-dāru-patra-ajina-mṛd-bhāṇḍa-a;sma-bhāṇḍa-vaidalebhyaḥ ṣāṣṭha-bhāgaṃ rājā //
VisnuSm 3-26 brāhmaṇebhyaḥ kara-ādānaṃ na kuryāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-27 te hi rāj;no dharma-karāḥ //
VisnuSm 3-28 rājā ca prajābhyaḥ su-kṛta-duṣ-kṛtebhyaḥ ṣaṣṭha-aṃ;sa-bhāk //
VisnuSm 3-29 sva-de;sa-paṇyāc ca ;sulka-aṃ;saṃ da;samam ādadyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-30 para-de;sa-paṇyāc ca viṃ;satitamam //
VisnuSm 3-31 ;sulka-sthānād apākrāman sarva-apahāram āpnuyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-32 ;silpinaḥ karma-jīvina;s ca māsena^ekaṃ rāj;naḥ karma kuryuḥ\ //
VisnuSm 3-33 svāmy-amātya-durga-ko;sa-daṇḍa-rāṣṭra-mitrāṇi prakṛtayaḥ //
VisnuSm 3-34 tad-dūṣakāṃ;s ca hanyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-35 sva-rāṣṭra-para-rāṣṭrayo;s ca cāra-cakṣuḥ syāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-36 sādhūnāṃ pūjanaṃ kuryāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-37 duṣṭāṃ;s ca hanyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-38 ;satru-mitra-udāsīna-madhyameṣu sāma-bheda-dāna-daṇḍān yathā-arhaṃ  yathā-kālaṃ prayu;njīta\ //
VisnuSm 3-39 saṃdhi-vigraha-yāna-āsana-saṃ;sraya-dvaidhī-bhāvāṃ;s ca yathā-kālam ā;srayet\ //
VisnuSm 3-40 caitre mārga;sīrṣe vā yātrāṃ yāyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-41 parasya vyasane vā //
VisnuSm 3-42 para-de;sa-avāptau tad-de;sa-dharmān na^ucchindyāt\ //
VisnuSm 3-43 pareṇa^abhiyukta;s ca sarva-ātmanā sva-rāṣṭraṃ gopāyet\ //
VisnuSm 3-44 na^asti\ rāj;nāṃ samare tanu-tyāga-sadṛ;so dharmaḥ //
VisnuSm 3-45 go-brāhmaṇa-nṛpa-mitra-dhana-dāra-jīvita-rakṣaṇāt ye hatās te svarga-loka-bhājaḥ //
VisnuSm 3-46 varṇa-saṃkara-rakṣaṇa-arthaṃ ca //
VisnuSm 3-47 rājā para-pura-avāptau tatra tat-kulīnam abhiṣi;ncet\ //
VisnuSm 3-48 na rāja-kulam ucchindyāt\ //

  VisnuSm_97
VisnuSm 97-01 ūru-stha-uttāna-caraṇaḥ savye kare karam itaraṃ nyasya\ tālu-stha-acala-jihvo dantair dantān a-saṃspṛ;san svaṃ nāsikā-agraṃ pa;syan di;sa;s ca^an-avalokayan vibhīḥ pra;sānta-ātmā caturviṃ;satyā tattvair vyatītaṃ cintayet\ /

  VisnuSm_99
VisnuSm 99-01a/ dṛṣṭvā\ ;sriyaṃ deva-devasya viṣṇor gṛhīta-pādāṃ tapasā jvalantīm /
